/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-477-
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-478-
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-479-Trigger Sequence
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-480-----------------
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-481-
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-482-Init uses the following sequence of triggers during early boot. These are the
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-483-built-in triggers defined in init.cpp.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-484-
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-485-   1. `early-init` - The first in the sequence, triggered after cgroups has been configured
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-486-      but before ueventd's coldboot is complete.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-487-   2. `init` - Triggered after coldboot is complete.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-488-   3. `charger` - Triggered if `ro.bootmode == "charger"`.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md:489:   4. `late-init` - Triggered if `ro.bootmode != "charger"`, or via healthd triggering a boot
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-490-      from charging mode.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-491-
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-492-Remaining triggers are configured in `init.rc` and are not built-in. The default sequence for
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-493-these is specified under the "on late-init" event in `init.rc`. Actions internal to `init.rc`
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-494-have been omitted.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-495-
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-496-   1. `early-fs` - Start vold.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-497-   2. `fs` - Vold is up. Mount partitions not marked as first-stage or latemounted.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-498-   3. `post-fs` - Configure anything dependent on early mounts.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-499-   4. `late-fs` - Mount partitions marked as latemounted.
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-500-   5. `post-fs-data` - Mount and configure `/data`; set up encryption. `/metadata` is
/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2/system/core/init/README.md-501-      reformatted here if it couldn't mount in first-stage init.
